> *In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/65fb75da-926a-456d-b33a-2551189c8a11?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)*

##### **Ben Stiller might make you laugh, and now he can quench your thirst too.**

The man has [launched a soda brand](https://substack.com/redirect/8735083f-a6a0-4e1a-b4de-6e642fcd4323?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), fittingly (unoriginally?) named Stiller’s. Though many media outlets have been framing this as a Better-For-You soda, the co-founder Alexander Doman said that Stiller’s sits somewhere between Big Soda (Coke, Pepsi) and Less Big Prebiotic Soda (Poppi, Ollipop).

Essentially, Stiller’s is born out of identifying a white space that exists in the category: a healthier soda that still has a classic, sugary taste. “Healthy soda for the soda lover.” If they were a Tracksuit customer, they could find that white space using [Tracksuit’s category profile](https://substack.com/redirect/7a526a68-0229-4066-9db0-5a25f426a764?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), which gives you a bird’s eye view of the demographic distribution of your potential buyers – and importantly, gaps other players in the category aren’t filling. 

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!zHDg!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd2714016-4f0c-4bce-9d74-64feee00597d_883x522.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/771dd8eb-6b63-45b3-b00e-3b857e0a39cf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)Soda is a crowded space, so finding that unique consumer wedge isn’t easy. In the US **Better-For-You soda category** that we track, the top two brands by awareness (by far) is Poppi and Olipop, both of which have experienced incredible growth in the last year. Poppi’s awareness increased from 45% to 66% between Sep ‘24 to Aug ‘25 (that’s 15 million more people) and Olipop increased from 38% to 53%.

As to the success of Stiller’s, it’s clear that like most celebrity brands, Awareness will be supercharged by stardom, but how they convert will still come down to other tried and true factors: pricing, availability, messaging, product, etc.

While we’ve got you here, [Miranda from Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/2a7f75fc-0f40-4071-8a7e-9fb7df31b72c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) has put together a quick video of how you can use the dashboard to make a big, bold campaign bet, and prove it worked.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!sjUU!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa21cfcaf-a721-407c-a9db-778b1aff1e95_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/033676cd-a518-4fba-9bda-cd7798f1b029?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* TikTok deal done and it’s somehow the [shittiest possible outcome](https://substack.com/redirect/de3791e6-5b56-40c2-b786-ba366e5cd965?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), making everything worse.
* ByteDance confirms TikTok will be [controlled by US owners](https://substack.com/redirect/70780808-399c-4066-a43e-fd65f52797e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Hack reveals the [a16z-backed phone farm flooding](https://substack.com/redirect/2445d54e-baef-4d32-b9ab-cc6a65793fe9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) TikTok with AI Influencers.
	+ TikTok [accused](https://substack.com/redirect/b42016d3-e532-4d84-9aba-112b42de5c7f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of aiding fake [AI-generated ads](https://substack.com/redirect/692e0b73-ae99-4ffb-8c53-a2a5d84602f8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on its platform.
* [TikTok](https://substack.com/redirect/df906b4f-8bbf-4ed9-b3cc-369ac2835744?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) tracked [user’s Grindr activity](https://substack.com/redirect/119f0ead-2f5d-4bff-9207-120035e45297?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in violation of European law, rights group alleges.
* TikTok adds more [accessibility](https://substack.com/redirect/11140f24-23ed-48ef-b189-466dafede890?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) features.
* Inside [U.S. TikTok Awards](https://substack.com/redirect/3d104fbc-d6a7-44f8-9d9b-6930b5220691?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): Paris Hilton, labubu cannon and tech issues.
	+ [Keith Lee](https://substack.com/redirect/1a643f20-73ae-4ea2-9e1b-4281bface1fa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) takes top prize at first TikTok Awards in US.
* TikTok is planning [a big 5-day RTO](https://substack.com/redirect/9ce6bb77-8464-45e3-9ca1-95317d920580?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) push next year.
* TikTok is rolling out the ability to [link fundraisers](https://substack.com/redirect/48d971c2-29b4-418b-86fd-24d64373b8aa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) directly within the app.
* How [Dr. Noc](https://substack.com/redirect/c1227228-d64f-4989-8c5b-c23538b03a8e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) made science work on TikTok.
* TikTok launches [self-service advertising platform](https://substack.com/redirect/3e3eb753-3362-41b9-8d84-6c13f8fe44fb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for small businesses in Pakistan.
* ‘[Supergirl](https://substack.com/redirect/1a9d4735-d6ce-4fdc-afcb-0ba4d70b1b89?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ takes over TikTok for a day.
* Here’s what that [red person icon](https://substack.com/redirect/895fccfa-d223-4675-8e14-e14262b8f65c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) means on TikTok, which appears next to usernames.

> TikTok’s mental health ‘[rabbit hole](https://substack.com/redirect/1ccaa83c-ea3e-4b53-916f-0108c9a1c012?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’? It’s not in your head.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!dE8f!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fc0b0e67a-ad8c-4778-a9ee-8066b4dda5be_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/6ebc62ff-e001-435d-879d-09631f8e3b09?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Instagram introduces Reels to the big screen with [Instagram for TV](https://substack.com/redirect/2af6ede3-126c-4b17-b21a-486a3c13c0ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) launching this week on [Fire TV devices](https://substack.com/redirect/8d4c17de-a555-4814-889f-bf3d9e0a35c6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Meta’s “[Trusted Experts](https://substack.com/redirect/49bfc8d3-20b8-47e1-a2cf-fe28fa9f7b5e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” helped me run scam ads on Facebook and Instagram.
* Threads adds [new communities](https://substack.com/redirect/2e8da36a-bcfc-4529-b1b6-4db0cb5d44f5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), tests badges for highly engaged members.
* [Edits App](https://substack.com/redirect/575cdac8-8c71-4fdc-b247-10385f1c908b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) now allows you to highlight, label, and add creative touches to specific people or objects in your videos.
	+ The platform also launched [new updates](https://substack.com/redirect/71c2e14d-c70c-4d6c-bc2c-38d3d7223081?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to titles, text presets, and New Year features.
* Why [fashion influencers](https://substack.com/redirect/9fc766ed-2828-4115-a721-9a81c3ea4c1e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are migrating from Instagram to Substack.
* Threads on iOS is testing an [updated screenshot watermark](https://substack.com/redirect/dab866c2-e7e8-440d-97fd-8979a27fbc75?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that includes not just the logo, but also the app name.
* Instagram expands “[Auto Scroll](https://substack.com/redirect/75f86182-e7fe-46b0-b7b7-2e1b4bb60fbd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” Reels feature to [more users](https://substack.com/redirect/7162ce18-a565-4079-93f8-33a6d45f22f4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Threads wants to be the [app you can’t wait to open](https://substack.com/redirect/dfa1279e-d830-417b-ba2d-10a137f0ddc4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in the morning.
	+ The fediverse is on maintenance mode!

> 🦀 Report Break: Gen Z went to the movies [an average of 6.1 times](https://substack.com/redirect/05c38733-89fa-458c-b101-f7fde2ff0887?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) (up from 4.9 times year-over-year), the highest number among age groups.

> But Gen Z isn’t the only group seeing films in theaters: 77% of Americans aged 12-74 saw at least one movie this year, representing a total of more than 200 million. From 2024 to 2025, new subscribers for cinema loyalty programs such as AMC Stubs, Cinemark Movie Rewards or Regal Crown Club, grew 15%.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VXYk!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F54241e92-dc00-45e3-b0a1-422988e5b58f_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/8462e6ed-9b84-4709-a1c7-9108db73aeb0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Meta tolerates [rampant ad fraud from China](https://substack.com/redirect/b537015d-720e-48a3-9665-b2e473d4a474?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to safeguard billions in revenue.
* Meta adopts [new age-check system](https://substack.com/redirect/f087102a-7882-455a-8ba3-271bafaac891?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to meet global child safety laws.
* Facebook [Messenger’s desktop app](https://substack.com/redirect/7619ac2e-1529-4dd3-a797-d5437ffd413a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is no more.
* Facebook is now showing “[Recently added X Friends](https://substack.com/redirect/b0621d71-c4d2-49e2-9b51-76ba827f00c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” on profiles in the “People You May Know” section.
* Meta’s scam problem may trigger [a legal reckoning](https://substack.com/redirect/af9db2ef-7027-4ce4-b648-5ee2a4b5f96f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Austria’s highest court [prohibits](https://substack.com/redirect/bcf93d6d-1923-4f31-ae42-97602bc6b933?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Meta Platforms’ business model.
* Two families sue Meta over [teens’ deaths](https://substack.com/redirect/45e410a2-3ead-43f7-ad2b-a924ce4140c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) by suicide, citing ‘sextortion’ scams.
* IRS takes [a new shot](https://substack.com/redirect/b4cb4274-a2b0-4725-ab30-ebf2b3cc45d9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at Meta’s foreign tax strategy.
* WhatsApp testing new feature that lets channel admins create [quiz-based updates](https://substack.com/redirect/8fa12363-7448-451f-be0d-4d8a6c8d437e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and share them with their followers.
	+ Indian WhatsApp tutors are teaching [ordinary people](https://substack.com/redirect/bb4c0b2a-b207-459d-9b6f-1d450d134bf8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) how to use AI.
* WhatsApp tests a new “[Invite followers](https://substack.com/redirect/f379baf3-bf19-44d4-b023-6ee0eb6532f1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” feature for broadcast channels.
	+ WhatsApp is widely rolling out [group member tags](https://substack.com/redirect/db608af3-ca2b-4e0c-8877-b746704d5f60?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on iOS.

* ElevenLabs partners with [Meta](https://substack.com/redirect/dd7fc6c5-fd32-4b4f-80fb-3fd1216a8d97?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for scalable audio solutions.
* Meta [announces](https://substack.com/redirect/497ff7d3-aa3b-475e-9b7f-82833e6b5b2a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) new updates to [Meta AI Glasses](https://substack.com/redirect/a66423ed-903a-4a31-a3b0-4b97d536d778?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): conversation focus, Spotify integration, and more.
* Meta is developing [a new image and video model](https://substack.com/redirect/31cf55b2-aa01-4d0c-924c-3b0b0ef5003c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for a 2026 release, report says.
* Meta’s Yann LeCun targets [€3bn valuation](https://substack.com/redirect/bbe1436b-5c16-4b3b-ab27-69cd7bec2511?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for AI start-up.
* Meta introduces [SAM Audio](https://substack.com/redirect/f03ed63f-d2e2-4e22-9c07-7d095f1c46e5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), a state-of-the-art AI model that enables you to segment sound.
* [Meta AI](https://substack.com/redirect/0177136c-ca23-452a-82a4-ac22bba067f1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to get Memories and Custom Prompts soon.
* Meta [pauses](https://substack.com/redirect/a2af7a9a-1dd7-4e46-9ea1-4a1e13268c8d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) third-party Horizon VR headsets program.
* Meta is making ‘[AI core to how we work](https://substack.com/redirect/ca2ebf5e-eeb6-4fa8-917a-22941b03fdad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ with the help of tools from Google and OpenAI.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!xdrk!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F504f7135-86f7-4edd-a923-b08f71d3eeb3_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/4b949717-5dcd-4a70-be07-a6f7ffd397e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* YouTube is Instagram: The platform is now testing [image posts](https://substack.com/redirect/db176d1d-c611-44ce-b6a8-b444e946df26?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in the Shorts feed.
* YouTube is letting creators make [playable games](https://substack.com/redirect/54813642-e19f-4d5f-94b7-a9c6b589d8da?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with a Gemini 3 tool.
	+ [YouTube](https://substack.com/redirect/3d852604-8103-4cac-bf81-cef47881b3f1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) expands AI Portraits to turn creators into [chatbots](https://substack.com/redirect/b5e00cda-e2bb-426c-9eb0-ac7d4cea61f3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* WPP Media enriches [influencer offering](https://substack.com/redirect/da02cf25-eb29-4dd4-b124-1933e0cd3931?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with YouTube creator data deal.
* YouTube protests [Billboard’s new formula](https://substack.com/redirect/d7d8110d-c9e9-43bf-a8ac-a15a2cb40c60?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) by pulling data from music charts.
* YouTube expands [Voice Replies](https://substack.com/redirect/f5dfffe5-f18f-4a94-aa36-2f04ce4eb84b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and launches YT Create on iOS, Super Chat Goals on vertical livestreams, and more.
* YouTube’s [channel guidelines](https://substack.com/redirect/cd7d42a7-e00e-4883-a7ed-21e66c531efb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are expanding to Communities and more creators.
* Exclusive: YouTube launches option for U.S. creators to [receive stablecoin payouts](https://substack.com/redirect/6e9caed5-1b95-4291-ba4b-e95476bed00c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) through PayPal.
* YouTube touts [TV podcast viewership](https://substack.com/redirect/af43df9c-2e2f-4afe-94ba-3cc1b28904e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)–right after Netflix poached its shows.
	+ People watched [700 million hours](https://substack.com/redirect/4015fc84-8421-41a6-a471-22ac36c4c1f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of YouTube podcasts on TV in October.
* *Google* Play and *YouTube* movies will now show up as part of your *[Movies Anywhere](https://substack.com/redirect/fa44e3f6-12e2-42d8-bf6f-34914dd056db?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* collection again.
* YouTube made [its video player](https://substack.com/redirect/647b46ed-50e7-4868-8e3a-df101b207b29?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) easier to navigate on TVs.
* In Graphic Detail: Why [YouTube](https://substack.com/redirect/ea63edf9-f8bc-458d-a7f7-15c1c3980c67?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is a genuine threat to Netflix.
* YouTube bans two popular channels that created [fake AI movie trailers](https://substack.com/redirect/cb6c1870-0352-46ce-9167-3baaf9850180?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ Google pulls [AI-generated videos of Disney characters](https://substack.com/redirect/bc7eca8d-b54e-4cf2-807a-3d71fd731321?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from YouTube in response to cease and desist.
* YouTube looks to streamline [sponsored content partnerships](https://substack.com/redirect/5d40d492-f8cd-4640-a859-3395fc7594fa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

> 🫒 Oxford Road's report highlights [a 25% gap in podcast video ad performance](https://substack.com/redirect/2bb0c6e7-56d7-4101-8a4e-c4db4b5e6180?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). The study shows that YouTube views are 18-25% less effective than audio downloads at driving purchases. 

> The result is that for every $1M spent on YouTube podcast impressions, brand advertisers may be losing up to $250k in conversion value.

> 🫩 Report Break: 87% of organizations report their [marketing investments yield unreliable or inflated intent signals](https://substack.com/redirect/f0733ddc-05f9-4bde-a446-86c94890867b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), such as clicks, downloads, and behavioral scores. Only 26% of “intent” signals convert to qualified opportunities. 66% of leaders say their campaign metrics often look successful but fail to drive actual revenue outcomes.

> Related to content creation inefficiencies, 72% of marketing leaders surveyed say AI-generated content is hurting brand distinction.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!85wj!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F20a7b272-bbeb-4fe3-a619-d76e41822174_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/d5e2b0bc-8fa8-4f13-9935-bf7662a14a08?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Snapchat’s new [Quick Cut stitches](https://substack.com/redirect/ffc4ce39-caa9-4792-b21e-3ceb6db21120?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) a video together for you.
	+ [Snapchat](https://substack.com/redirect/66724da3-7251-4f38-a0da-f10d570f084b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) rolls out its end-of-year Recaps.
	+ Snap is using [Apple’s age range measure](https://substack.com/redirect/1173f0d8-ffc0-4f9d-ae52-50d9309114cb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to restrict underage users.
	+ Snapchat publishes [new research](https://substack.com/redirect/3eb396d4-4f00-46fa-8ccf-f1061e2af6bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on how brands can successfully utilize visual, 1:1 communication.
		- Brand DMs can shift consumer perception in meaningful ways. 65% of consumers report a positive shift in their opinions of brands that message them in DMs.
* Reddit is arguing it’s a ‘[collection of public fora](https://substack.com/redirect/b4750754-8dbf-4418-bf9e-f2176d09f120?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ and not a social media company. Here’s why.
	+ AI-generated content a triple threat for Reddit moderators. ([Study](https://substack.com/redirect/d06d66a4-148d-4cb6-829a-0dca7ce4ea92?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g))
* WPP Media intergrates [Pinterest’s Trends](https://substack.com/redirect/aa8b44ae-93bb-4a90-81b9-14d7a050ccaf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into WPP Open.
	+ Pinterest informs affected users about [Shuffles app](https://substack.com/redirect/d22a7a37-b290-4a63-88ad-36191847a0bd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) data breach.
* Bluesky is introducing [Find Friends](https://substack.com/redirect/aae44abf-dfd0-4059-92ad-ba8318d7a850?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) — a privacy-first contact import feature.
* Twitch introduces [Lead Moderators](https://substack.com/redirect/ad8a5435-0031-44bd-abde-3337028b4031?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): a new role that helps streamers manage their growing communities
	+ [Kick CEO](https://substack.com/redirect/de417305-2d5d-4092-a891-e643c9010c6d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) confirms the platform will [turn on ads](https://substack.com/redirect/266a9acf-a87c-40bb-8d74-ef1f63b439af?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) “at some point.”
* The Wall Street Journal joins Substack with “[Free Expression](https://substack.com/redirect/6b4ff95c-fc56-48bb-a984-08d1b56e4e6a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g),” a newsletter-focused opinion brand.
* Amazon changes how [copyright protection](https://substack.com/redirect/9f893b2f-f3f2-497f-9580-56cfe84f9808?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is applied to Kindle Direct’s self-published e-books.
* Mozilla says [Firefox will evolve into an AI browser](https://substack.com/redirect/d1832e4f-dc96-4673-9aaf-95ebc7515c93?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), and nobody is happy about it — “I’ve never seen a company so astoundingly out of touch.”
	+ Firefox responds to AI backlash by promising a ‘[kill switch](https://substack.com/redirect/8ce661d6-bee6-4eba-bbc6-474729903430?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ for turning off controversial new features.
* [Hackers](https://substack.com/redirect/5e5ed5c2-9e26-4962-853c-94ba1bcd97de?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) access Pornhub’s premium users’ viewing habits and search history.
* Strava puts popular “[Year in Sport](https://substack.com/redirect/93726a56-cbf0-4bd7-8a04-d0fdedd7e06b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” recap behind an $80 paywall.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!pHUV!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F18a9aabb-0c0d-4ebe-8d32-7ad73a812d1c_2008x1284.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/b83010ab-92ce-480f-a87e-a376b7ba7e13?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)
> 🤨 Report Break: Due to content saturation, Instagram’s reach continues to decline. [Metricool’s 2026 Social Media study](https://substack.com/redirect/b83010ab-92ce-480f-a87e-a376b7ba7e13?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shows that reach fell by 35% for Reels, alongside an overall 31% drop in post reach across the platform.

> Meanwhile, TikTok’s reach continues to grow. Despite a slight decline in posting frequency, TikTok remains the platform with the highest average reach (28,482) and the greatest growth potential, with more than 17% of accounts increasing their follower counts.

> Something interesting to note is that posting frequency on Pinterest grew by 29%, while LinkedIn is in decline in terms of both posting frequency and impressions.

* DuckDuckGo’s [privacy-first AI platform](https://substack.com/redirect/c47d8845-d0a3-431d-8ad5-5e53d780a543?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) can now generate images.
* Peacock showing [ads upon launch](https://substack.com/redirect/cefca44e-925d-4f9a-bdd6-54a06c5aaf1a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) opens the door for more disruptive streaming ads.
* [Roblox’s 2025 Replay](https://substack.com/redirect/771d7ce3-7640-4535-a205-ab54a8d15ee6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shows just how obsessed Gen Z is with the game.
	+ Why brands are still showing up on [Roblox](https://substack.com/redirect/83d98f25-76a2-4331-962c-175490bb562b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Nothing’s lockscreen ‘[ads](https://substack.com/redirect/cdeb0caa-cd6e-44c4-9d03-7c526d86b67d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ seem to wreck battery life if you try to fully remove the app.
* [Kajabi](https://substack.com/redirect/8c2f54f2-7f8f-4d51-b56d-b6f858780dcf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is now offering creator financing, citing reports that 81% of small biz owners struggle to access capital.
* Brian Lawlor goes all in on building [Scripps Sports’](https://substack.com/redirect/e8c4365d-0ea8-4646-bbe3-359c0cf7151a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) broad reach.
* Caleb Hearon’s ‘So True’ Podcast inks exclusive [distribution & advertising deal](https://substack.com/redirect/0bfe5975-b73b-4e94-9079-c6cbaef95214?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Wave.
* [Apple Safari](https://substack.com/redirect/a31b4590-b7bc-43e8-a838-809c8d98b868?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) update enables tracking two core web vitals metrics.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!1wLZ!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5a067f43-c54c-4bfd-9047-817140dde343_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/23c8ede4-8513-4167-af7b-0ea870ac91e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* We let [AI run our office](https://substack.com/redirect/bb2f2f15-c8be-4e0a-8ba7-15a013d48a78?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) vending machine. It [lost](https://substack.com/redirect/f109c2cc-9227-4a60-9ec9-153d63b02a33?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) hundreds of dollars.
* [AI Chatbots](https://substack.com/redirect/d594f0cc-469a-4dad-96b8-6ec95efd2cf7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are poisoning research archives with fake citations.
	+ People are paying to get their chatbots high on ‘[drugs](https://substack.com/redirect/8cda2265-23ba-4eea-9b07-d2515c4ec557?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).’
* Anthropic’s [AI vending machine](https://substack.com/redirect/f109c2cc-9227-4a60-9ec9-153d63b02a33?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) turns communist and gives everything for free.
* We [mapped](https://substack.com/redirect/88871777-6c34-4a85-ad50-541987163914?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the world’s hottest data centers.
* [UPS](https://substack.com/redirect/f83ec918-d15c-49b7-acd3-e6d5c98b8508?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) tests AI tool to spot fake returns.
* Publishers are hunting for [AI prompt data](https://substack.com/redirect/213d2af1-9612-444b-8921-e7f498fc9207?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) — now they’re starting to get it from third-party companies.
	+ [Major publishers](https://substack.com/redirect/0e065e91-5534-4eeb-a394-850d8c4fb0ff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) back universal AI licensing technology.
* Accenture and Palantir [expand](https://substack.com/redirect/5cc71a56-43b5-4635-8a16-1aaf7401a894?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) global strategic partnership.
	+ Accenture restructuring costs soar to [$923M](https://substack.com/redirect/b2fb0cff-e986-40cd-842a-8ace713ba67e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) amid AI pivot.
* [Luma AI](https://substack.com/redirect/81d26ca4-550f-4584-b4c1-7a126b0f6aae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) working on Realms, Ray 3 Flash and Image V2.
* Anthropic launches enterprise ‘[Agent Skills](https://substack.com/redirect/460e037d-8370-4116-86cf-c8af46707b07?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ and opens the standard, challenging OpenAI in workplace AI.
* [OpenAI has a problem](https://substack.com/redirect/1e79fff3-7839-43f0-9d84-00b0c0457214?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and, unfortunately for it, that problem is also how it makes most of its money: ChatGPT.
	+ OpenAI is reportedly trying to [raise $100B](https://substack.com/redirect/21690ad4-b44a-436c-bea0-4c5d1b88a9bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at an $830B valuation.

	+ The [ChatGPT app store](https://substack.com/redirect/c486d6f4-cc1a-4e61-99f3-2de434e83749?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is here.
	+ ChatGPT’s [Apple Music integration](https://substack.com/redirect/39cfd05e-dfe2-47b7-af7c-a0131f498173?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is now live.
	+ You’ll soon lose access to [ChatGPT’s Voice feature](https://substack.com/redirect/ff3595d3-07ef-4751-9dcb-109a711b83a7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on macOS.
* Salesforce buys [Qualified](https://substack.com/redirect/207d4331-4429-41fc-9be6-5f2a64e5b62e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to accelerate its AI‑driven GTM strategy.
	+ Salesforce launches an [Agentforce Sales app](https://substack.com/redirect/b9cbb421-72d7-4c80-bfec-be6bf4472395?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for ChatGPT.
* Google Cloud introduces new AI tool ‘The Core’ in collaboration with [Al Jazeera](https://substack.com/redirect/9ee18e55-3337-45f9-a4a6-63ce6161510c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Beam launches [AI-powered platform](https://substack.com/redirect/bf08537e-4266-4d44-a41c-3f7aa348ada1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for making playable videos.
* [Databricks](https://substack.com/redirect/5b0d54e3-c9ef-4936-9420-22102246f61d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is raising funds at $134 Billion valuation.
* Waymo seeking over [$15 billion](https://substack.com/redirect/859bb9ae-894e-413d-887b-d169eed34f30?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) near $100 billion valuation.
* Adobe Firefly now supports [prompt-based video editing](https://substack.com/redirect/03e34a1a-bb69-4b23-9bdf-4814ccf4cd6e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), adds more third-party models.
* OpenAI’s [new ChatGPT image generator](https://substack.com/redirect/035650df-f0af-4282-95a7-ffd5e5d2e7ed?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) makes faking photos easy.

> 🥀 Report Break: Anthropic had the highest [crawl-to-refer ratio](https://substack.com/redirect/6186171a-2aa3-4b84-9694-6c30c1ee5001?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) among the leading AI and search platforms. 

> Meanwhile Perplexity had the lowest crawl-to-refer ratios of the major AI platforms, starting the year below 100:1 before spiking in late March above 700:1, concurrent with a spike of crawl traffic seen from PerplexityBot.

* ‘AI is going to automate the ordinary’: Cindy Rose’s plan for ad giant [WPP](https://substack.com/redirect/b5db8ed6-cf6f-44ef-9221-71b56ad091b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).

---

* Clinch introduces [Predict IQ Scores™](https://substack.com/redirect/400ed346-4a5e-41e3-a200-22c579bac730?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to help advertisers validate creative effectiveness pre-launch.
* 80% of [UK Linear TV spend](https://substack.com/redirect/85399b7b-e962-4695-9b8c-128da9f4ade7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is leaving TV.
	+ According to Guideline data, out of every pound that leaves linear TV, only 21p is reinvested in CTV, with social (19 percent), search (17 percent) and retail media networks (15 percent) all taking a slice from linear TV budgets.
* At [Ebiquity](https://substack.com/redirect/94174036-de8c-4ece-8434-084ff7fc4a03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), a new role signals marketing’s shift from metrics to meaning.
* NBCU [democratizes](https://substack.com/redirect/f3e864d8-45b6-4318-b8f7-194242c25e30?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) access to Olympics for second straight Games.
* Fixated secures [$50 million](https://substack.com/redirect/7bab346a-e9ee-4b8e-a89d-e89eb6eee5de?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to build vertically-integrated creator and content machine.
* Texas sues biggest TV makers, alleging [smart TVs spy on users](https://substack.com/redirect/8358ca1b-7016-41a1-bb4c-dee9d80d0f9a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) without consent.
* impact.com partners with Evertune to strengthen [AI Search visibility](https://substack.com/redirect/a8e06996-2064-4103-a449-fa7dbd9ff2e6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for affiliates.
* [AppLovin](https://substack.com/redirect/80a90606-3df4-45f9-98e4-8d792265729e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is up 7.6% after analysts spotlight AXON Ads and AI Ad stack momentum.
* Cvent buys [Goldcast](https://substack.com/redirect/a64aabd5-09fa-4b80-a22f-87dee9ccd7af?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to create event and video marketing platform.
* PwC U.K. is giving Gen Z grads ‘[resilience](https://substack.com/redirect/fed1ce93-6454-4510-be5e-f19c60d69102?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ training in their first 6 months on the job, to get better at handling criticisms and office politics.
* Can [corporate events](https://substack.com/redirect/00310291-880d-4c1c-9cf4-69a17d8e0051?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) be sexy? This IPO-bound company thinks so.
* Albertsons’ new tech allows shoppers to turn [offsite media](https://substack.com/redirect/a7f93795-9d9e-4f47-97c9-f73d2c1d2adb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into purchases.

> 🤔 Report Break: Global Internet traffic [grew 19% in 2025](https://substack.com/redirect/6186171a-2aa3-4b84-9694-6c30c1ee5001?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), with significant growth starting in August. Google and Facebook once again held the top two spots among the top 10. Microsoft, Instagram, and YouTube all moved higher; Amazon Web Services (AWS) dropped one spot lower, while TikTok fell four spots.

> [![BLOG-3077 3 - traffic-internet traffic growth - worldwide](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!njHB!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd0f451fc-e206-4653-bbbb-5444ad366c2b_1600x898.png "BLOG-3077 3 - traffic-internet traffic growth - worldwide")](https://substack.com/redirect/165ebe5e-d49b-40ad-b2a4-f9ac6623cda7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: Spectral,sans-serif;font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;"><span>This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/41f43461-08ac-4e7d-a696-cd97e4e314d1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard. </span><em>Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.</em></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/bec55781","text":"The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)","action":null,"class":"button-wrapper"}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/30d867d4-4328-49ec-9cac-4e9cb0a43236?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;border: none;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">The Social Juice+ (more of what matters)</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="728"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f67276fc-e43e-4ce2-9561-4b604e27b345?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/e2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":728,"bytes":50683,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":true,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!VaNH!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok signs Trump-backed deal to avoid </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cb54f25d-7be1-4a3f-af31-e5e17d6927ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">US ban</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Facebook tests </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2813f3de-344b-40d4-9059-6e92aab559a8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">charging users to share links</a><span> in potential blow for news outlets.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">News publishers aren’t included in the ongoing test, but it could soon reach them.</p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7de4d4db-3172-4bd2-b179-0f0031f37271?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ChatGPT</a><span> will now let you pick how nice it is.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b7510be0-b37c-4895-86b5-482b4fbdafad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Netflix</a><span> strikes video podcast deal with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2a7bc849-0e6b-46a0-8ba2-7340a2ea15e8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Barstool Sports</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram limits </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a79c6de2-116a-4357-89c6-14083fb86295?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">number of hashtags</a><span> to five per post.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/db7342d5-e7c2-404c-a4fb-dafd2b907ad3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Oscars</a><span> moving to YouTube beginning in 2029, will stream free worldwide.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d4a54942-0127-4039-9bc3-d956a83ce69d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":48232,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!zM4F!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ecda0d8c-eab3-454b-891e-01afc34251b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Amazon</a><span> in talks to invest more than $10bn in OpenAI.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>U.S. threatens </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/067cc159-5845-4155-899b-855f3e3f537d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">penalties against European tech firms</a><span> amid regulatory fight.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The hypocrisy of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9f8df349-3128-468c-b5f4-af7fbb21e8d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">US complaints</a><span> over X’s EU fine.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f6c49cbf-821b-4ee5-a4d8-b05eec7d25e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Apple</a><span> announces </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0310e1a4-7577-4e3a-9ae6-49fda179753d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more ads</a><span> are coming to App Store search results.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Companies are desperately seeking ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e3ccc09a-fef2-4bf0-b31c-6d8dc4500cc5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">storytellers</a><span>.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Microsoft scales back AI goals because almost </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d854c276-aa11-4bf2-9f18-bd9a13444b5d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">nobody is using Copilot</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3e93c090-b34b-4e40-a5c5-39b1f163aa6e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">PayPal</a><span> applies to form its own bank to expand small-business lending.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d42b45aa-2e0a-4530-b592-4d96b401a40b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Sony</a><span> takes control of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a2a8f540-3392-4642-a27a-f48afce32b90?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Snoopy</a><span> in deal for majority of Peanuts brand.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Warner Bros Discovery </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/98b3ed8e-b995-43fc-bb5c-a1753bcb2762?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">rejects</a><span> Paramount’s hostile takeover bid.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Tracksuit: These 10 Brands Officially Got </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9c3432a2-dbff-4de4-9604-8d2e4be2de31?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Gen Z’s Attention</a><span> In 2025.\*\*</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Source at major TV network flags ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3061e2cd-48f3-45a6-888f-ba1639c3bf41?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">discrepancies</a><span>’ in Nielsen Big Data + Panel ratings.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f84fc5e8-cacc-4a91-90c1-67127ad31b9b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Nielsen</a><span> hits back after VAB says big data + panel has gone ‘worst-case-scenario.’</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>DraftKings launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e3dd3e19-29d7-426d-a0cb-7fad929e2bf4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">prediction market</a><span> amid fight over sports betting.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><em><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/65fb75da-926a-456d-b33a-2551189c8a11?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a></em></p></blockquote>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;"><strong>Ben Stiller might make you laugh, and now he can quench your thirst too.</strong></h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>The man has</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8735083f-a6a0-4e1a-b4de-6e642fcd4323?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> launched a soda brand</a><span>, fittingly (unoriginally?) named Stiller’s. Though many media outlets have been framing this as a Better-For-You soda, the co-founder Alexander Doman said that Stiller’s sits somewhere between Big Soda (Coke, Pepsi) and Less Big Prebiotic Soda (Poppi, Ollipop).</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Essentially, Stiller’s is born out of identifying a white space that exists in the category: a healthier soda that still has a classic, sugary taste. “Healthy soda for the soda lover.” If they were a Tracksuit customer, they could find that white space using </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7a526a68-0229-4066-9db0-5a25f426a764?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit’s category profile</a><span>, which gives you a bird’s eye view of the demographic distribution of your potential buyers – and importantly, gaps other players in the category aren’t filling. </span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="624"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/771dd8eb-6b63-45b3-b00e-3b857e0a39cf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/d2714016-4f0c-4bce-9d74-64feee00597d\_883x522.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":522,"width":883,"resizeWidth":624,"bytes":null,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":null,"href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":null,"isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="325.14156285390715" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!zHDg!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd2714016-4f0c-4bce-9d74-64feee00597d\_883x522.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Soda is a crowded space, so finding that unique consumer wedge isn’t easy. In the US </span><strong>Better-For-You soda category </strong><span>that we track, the top two brands by awareness (by far) is Poppi and Olipop, both of which have experienced incredible growth in the last year. Poppi’s awareness increased from 45% to 66% between Sep ‘24 to Aug ‘25 (that’s 15 million more people) and Olipop increased from 38% to 53%.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">As to the success of Stiller’s, it’s clear that like most celebrity brands, Awareness will be supercharged by stardom, but how they convert will still come down to other tried and true factors: pricing, availability, messaging, product, etc.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>While we’ve got you here, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2a7f75fc-0f40-4071-8a7e-9fb7df31b72c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Miranda from Tracksuit </a><span>has put together a quick video of how you can use the dashboard to make a big, bold campaign bet, and prove it worked. </span></p>
<p data-attrs='{"url":"https://hubs.li/Q03Wcr540","text":"See Tracksuit in action.","action":null,"class":null}' data-component-name="ButtonCreateButton" style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;text-align: center;cursor: pointer;border-radius: 4px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2a7f75fc-0f40-4071-8a7e-9fb7df31b72c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';display: inline-block;box-sizing: border-box;cursor: pointer;border: none;border-radius: 8px;font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 600;text-align: center;margin: 0;opacity: 1;outline: none;white-space: nowrap;color: #ffffff !important;text-decoration: none !important;background-color: #e85c57;padding: 12px 20px;height: auto;"><span style="color: #ffffff;text-decoration: none;">See Tracksuit in action.</span></a></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Podcasters love YouTube. That’s making </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/19b14cbf-8b04-4395-af35-837fa538e788?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">podcast advertising</a><span> less predictable.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/547586e1-2593-4c5e-9906-30b53cb635dc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Anti-Shein”</a><span> bandwagon gains momentum. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>A Reddit post helped find </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1905bebd-a713-4b3d-89e7-8b34c37a7a51?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">MIT and Brown shooting suspect.</a><span> Here’s what we know.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/365aac25-9ae3-4ecc-b749-7d4a5f487887?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Airbnb</a><span> fined £56m by Spain for advertising unlicensed properties.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c5e382e8-a205-4598-8aa4-dcfbd664b11a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Mixue</a><span> to open first store in LA as more Chinese food, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c5e382e8-a205-4598-8aa4-dcfbd664b11a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">beverage brands</a><span> tap into US market.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/04b9e132-eb69-4f25-948b-cff95fcfb8b1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">publicity war</a><span> between AT&amp;T and T-Mobile is getting ugly.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>2025 was the beginning of the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fc7be435-4080-4e17-aaa4-22a41e8e4cb3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">end of the TV brightness war</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dbca04cf-de1d-4204-bbbd-68a057176bf1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Trump Media</a><span> stock is surging on news that it’s merging with a fusion energy company in a $6 billion deal.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c257addd-b08e-409e-9196-9856b76873c9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Vanity Fair photographer</a><span> who disrupted Trumpworld’s polished image.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>BBC says it will defend </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6f137aec-12c7-4794-bcb4-e83e7a5db83e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Trump defamation lawsuit</a><span> over Panorama speech edit.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>White House investigates </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fc430ccf-fcd8-4192-8d86-0ee23d1a6a4d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">mystery video stream</a><span> on website.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/13aa2c41-d135-41f9-b5cd-9e5ed259a050?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">talented youth</a><span> nurtured the wrong way? Top performers develop </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5b429fac-f4de-4621-9b66-0d0cbecbdb54?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">differently</a><span> than assumed, says </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aad93dcb-e1d3-4806-be85-9daf5ceaa4b8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">study</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>How the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c7ca221c-0924-4640-9df8-3e46aeb2f1bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">phone ban</a><span> saved high school.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Diadora and Marathon Sports offering </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8219a984-b29d-429d-b874-4d0a763b7d73?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$40,000 NIL deal</a><span> to high schoolers.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram introduces Reels to the big screen with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2af6ede3-126c-4b17-b21a-486a3c13c0ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Instagram for TV</a><span> launching this week on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8d4c17de-a555-4814-889f-bf3d9e0a35c6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Fire TV devices</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta’s “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/49bfc8d3-20b8-47e1-a2cf-fe28fa9f7b5e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Trusted Experts</a><span>” helped me run scam ads on Facebook and Instagram.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Threads adds </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2e8da36a-bcfc-4529-b1b6-4db0cb5d44f5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new communities</a><span>, tests badges for highly engaged members.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/575cdac8-8c71-4fdc-b247-10385f1c908b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Edits App</a><span> now allows you to highlight, label, and add creative touches to specific people or objects in your videos.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The platform also launched </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/71c2e14d-c70c-4d6c-bc2c-38d3d7223081?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new updates</a><span> to titles, text presets, and New Year features.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Why </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9fc766ed-2828-4115-a721-9a81c3ea4c1e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">fashion influencers</a><span> are migrating from Instagram to Substack.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Threads on iOS is testing an </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dab866c2-e7e8-440d-97fd-8979a27fbc75?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">updated screenshot watermark</a><span> that includes not just the logo, but also the app name.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram expands “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/75f86182-e7fe-46b0-b7b7-2e1b4bb60fbd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Auto Scroll</a><span>” Reels feature to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7162ce18-a565-4079-93f8-33a6d45f22f4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more users</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Threads wants to be the</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dfa1279e-d830-417b-ba2d-10a137f0ddc4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> app you can’t wait to open</a><span> in the morning.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">The fediverse is on maintenance mode!</p></li></ul>
</li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🦀 Report Break: Gen Z went to the movies </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/05c38733-89fa-458c-b101-f7fde2ff0887?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">an average of 6.1 times</a><span> (up from 4.9 times year-over-year), the highest number among age groups.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">But Gen Z isn’t the only group seeing films in theaters: 77% of Americans aged 12-74 saw at least one movie this year, representing a total of more than 200 million. From 2024 to 2025, new subscribers for cinema loyalty programs such as AMC Stubs, Cinemark Movie Rewards or Regal Crown Club, grew 15%.</p>
</blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>

<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8462e6ed-9b84-4709-a1c7-9108db73aeb0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/54241e92-dc00-45e3-b0a1-422988e5b58f\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":34391,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F54241e92-dc00-45e3-b0a1-422988e5b58f\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!VXYk!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F54241e92-dc00-45e3-b0a1-422988e5b58f\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
